이제 `step` 메서드를 수정하겠습니다:

---

_**Assistant**_

```
async def step(self, action: np.ndarray):
    """Execute one time step within the environment"""
    # Convert action to order
    order_size = float(action[0])  # -1 to 1
    
    # Skip if no action
    if abs(order_size) < 0.1:
        obs = self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
            'position': self.position,
            'balance': self.balance
        }
        return obs, 0.0, False, False, info
    
    # Calculate order details
    side = "buy" if order_size > 0 else "sell"
    price = self._get_current_price()
    amount = abs(order_size) * self.balance / price
    
    try:
        # Place order
        order_id = await self.create_order(side, amount, price)
        
        # Wait for order to fill
        filled_amount = 0.0
        async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
            if status['status'] == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                filled_amount = status['filled']
                break
        
        # Update position and balance
        if side == "buy":
            self.position += filled_amount * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            self.balance -= filled_amount * price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
        else:
            self.position -= filled_amount
            self.balance += filled_amount * price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
        
        # Calculate reward
        new_portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (new_portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = new_portfolio_value
        
        # Remove filled order
        if order_id in self.active_orders:
            del self.active_orders[order_id]
        
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error executing trade: {e}")
        reward = -0.1  # Penalty for failed trade
    
    # Get observation and info
    obs = self._get_observation()
    info = {
        'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
        'position': self.position,
        'balance': self.balance
    }
    
    return obs, reward, False, False, info
```

                'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
                'price': 100.0,
                'volume': 1000.0,
                'bid': 99.5,
                'ask': 100.5,
                'bid_volume': 500.0,
                'ask_volume': 500.0
            })

    async def create_order(self, side: str, amount: float, price: float) -> str:
        """Create a new order"""
        try:
            if self.test_mode:
                order_id = "test_order"
                self.active_orders[order_id] = {
                    'id': order_id,
                    'side': side,
                    'amount': amount,
                    'price': price,
                    'filled': 0.0,  # Don't auto-fill in test mode
                    'status': OrderStatus.OPEN
                }
                return order_id

```
    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray):
        """Execute one time step within the environment"""
        # Convert action to order
        order_size = float(action[0])  # -1 to 1
        
        # Skip if no action
        if abs(order_size) < 0.1:
            obs = self._get_observation()
            info = {
                'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
                'position': self.position,
                'balance': self.balance
            }
            return obs, 0.0, False, False, info
        
        # Calculate order details
        side = "buy" if order_size > 0 else "sell"
        price = self._get_current_price()
        amount = abs(order_size) * self.balance / price
        
        try:
            # Place order
            order_id = await self.create_order(side, amount, price)
            
            # Wait for order to fill
            filled_amount = 0.0
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                    filled_amount = status['filled']
                    break
            
            # Update position and balance
            if filled_amount > 0:
                if side == "buy":
                    self.position += filled_amount * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                    self.balance -= filled_amount * price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= filled_amount
                    self.balance += filled_amount * price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                # Calculate reward
                new_portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
                reward = (new_portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
                self._last_portfolio_value = new_portfolio_value
            else:
                reward = -0.01  # Small penalty for unfilled order
            
            # Remove filled order
            if order_id in self.active_orders:
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error executing trade: {e}")
            reward = -0.1  # Penalty for failed trade
        
        # Get observation and info
        obs = self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
            'position': self.position,
            'balance': self.balance
        }
        
        return obs, reward, False, False, info
```

    async def monitor_order(self, order_id: str, timeout: float = 5.0) -> AsyncGenerator[Dict, None]:
        """Monitor order status until it's filled or canceled"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        while True:
            try:
                status = await self.check_order_status(order_id)
                yield status
                
                if status['status'] in [
                    OrderStatus.CLOSED,
                    OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    OrderStatus.EXPIRED,
                    OrderStatus.REJECTED
                ]:
                    break
                
                # Check timeout
                if (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() > timeout:
                    logger.warning(f"Order {order_id} monitoring timed out")
                    break
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.1)  # Reduced sleep time for testing
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error monitoring order {order_id}: {e}")
                break

    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray):
        """Execute one time step within the environment"""
        # Convert action to order
        order_size = float(action[0])  # -1 to 1
        
        # Skip if no action
        if abs(order_size) < 0.1:
            obs = self._get_observation()
            info = {
                'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
                'position': self.position,
                'balance': self.balance
            }
            return obs, 0.0, False, False, info
        
        # Calculate order details
        side = "buy" if order_size > 0 else "sell"
        price = self._get_current_price()
        amount = abs(order_size) * self.balance / price
        
        try:
            # Place order
            order_id = await self.create_order(side, amount, price)
            
            # Wait for order to fill
            filled_amount = 0.0
            order_status = None
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                order_status = status
                if status['status'] == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                    filled_amount = float(status['filled'])
                    break
            
            # Update position and balance
            if filled_amount > 0:
                if side == "buy":
                    self.position += filled_amount * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                    self.balance -= filled_amount * price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= filled_amount
                    self.balance += filled_amount * price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                # Calculate reward
                new_portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
                reward = (new_portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
                self._last_portfolio_value = new_portfolio_value
            else:
                reward = -0.01  # Small penalty for unfilled order
            
            # Remove filled order
            if order_id in self.active_orders:
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
            
        except ccxt.NetworkError:
            logger.error("Network error while executing trade")
            reward = -0.1
        except ccxt.ExchangeError as e:
            logger.error(f"Exchange error while executing trade: {e}")
            reward = -0.1
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error executing trade: {e}")
            reward = -0.1
        
        # Get observation and info
        obs = self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value,
            'position': self.position,
            'balance': self.balance
        }
        
        return obs, reward, False, False, info

```
class LiveTradingEnvironment(gym.Env):
    """Live Trading Environment that extends the base TradingEnvironment"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        symbol: str = "BTC/USDT",
        initial_balance: float = 10000.0,
        trading_fee: float = 0.001,
        window_size: int = 60,
        exchange_id: str = "binance",
        max_data_points: int = 1000,
        test_mode: bool = False,
        retry_attempts: int = 3,
        retry_delay: float = 1.0,
        websocket: Optional[WebSocketLoader] = None,
        exchange: Optional[ccxt.Exchange] = None,
    ):
        super(LiveTradingEnvironment, self).__init__()

        self.symbol = symbol
        self.test_mode = test_mode
        self.retry_attempts = retry_attempts
        self.retry_delay = retry_delay

        # Initialize exchange
        if exchange is not None:
            self.exchange = exchange
        elif not test_mode:
            self.exchange = getattr(ccxt, exchange_id)()
        
        # Initialize WebSocket loader
        if websocket is not None:
            self.websocket = websocket
        else:
            self.websocket = WebSocketLoader(
                exchange_id=exchange_id,
                symbol=symbol,
                max_data_points=max_data_points,
            )

        # Trading parameters
        self.initial_balance = initial_balance
        self.trading_fee = trading_fee
        self.window_size = window_size

        # Action and observation spaces
        self.action_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-1, high=1, shape=(1,), dtype=np.float32
        )
        n_features = 13  # Update to match actual feature count
        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-np.inf,
            high=np.inf,
            shape=(window_size, n_features),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        # Order management
        self.active_orders: Dict[str, Order] = {}
        self.filled_orders: List[Order] = []
        self.canceled_orders: List[Order] = []
        
        # Rate limiting
        self.last_api_call = datetime.min
        self.rate_limit_reset = 0.0
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()

    def _initialize_state(self):
        """Initialize environment state"""
        self.balance = self.initial_balance
        self.position = 0.0
        self.trades = []
        self.active_orders = {}
        self.canceled_orders = set()
        self._last_portfolio_value = self.initial_balance
        self._last_price = 100.0  # Initial price for test mode
        
        # Initialize mock data for testing
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()

    def _update_mock_data(self):
        """Update mock market data for testing"""
        if not self.test_mode:
            return
            
        # Simulate price movement
        price_change = np.random.normal(0, 1.0)
        self._last_price *= (1 + price_change * 0.01)
        
        self.websocket.add_mock_data({
            'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
            'price': self._last_price,
            'volume': 1000.0,
            'bid': self._last_price * 0.995,
            'ask': self._last_price * 1.005,
            'bid_volume': 500.0,
            'ask_volume': 500.0
        })

    @property
    def portfolio_value(self) -> float:
        """Calculate current portfolio value"""
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        position_value = self.position * current_price
        return self.balance + position_value

    async def create_order(self, side: str, amount: float, price: float) -> str:
        """Create a new order"""
        for attempt in range(self.retry_attempts):
            try:
                if self.test_mode:
                    order_id = f"test_order_{len(self.active_orders)}"
                    order = Order(
                        id=order_id,
                        symbol=self.symbol,
                        type="limit",
                        side=side,
                        amount=amount,
                        price=price,
                        status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                    )
                    self.active_orders[order_id] = order
                    return order_id
                
                # Call exchange API
                order = await self.exchange.create_order(
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price
                )
                
                # Store order details
                order_id = order['id']
                order_obj = Order(
                    id=order_id,
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price,
                    status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                )
                self.active_orders[order_id] = order_obj
                return order_id
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error creating order (attempt {attempt + 1}/{self.retry_attempts}): {e}")
                if attempt < self.retry_attempts - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self.retry_delay)
                else:
                    raise

    async def cancel_order(self, order_id: str) -> bool:
        """Cancel an order"""
        try:
            if self.test_mode:
                if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                    return False
                
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
                self.canceled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                return True
            
            # Call exchange API
            await self.exchange.cancel_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
            self.canceled_orders.append(order)
            del self.active_orders[order_id]
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error canceling order {order_id}: {e}")
            return False

    async def check_order_status(self, order_id: str) -> Dict:
        """Check the status of an order"""
        try:
            if self.test_mode:
                if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                    return {
                        'id': order_id,
                        'status': OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                        'filled': 0.0
                    }
                
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Simulate order filling in test mode
                if order.status == OrderStatus.OPEN:
                    current_price = self._last_price
                    if (order.side == 'buy' and current_price <= order.price) or \
                       (order.side == 'sell' and current_price >= order.price):
                        order.filled = order.amount
                        order.remaining = 0.0
                        order.status = OrderStatus.CLOSED
                        self.filled_orders.append(order)
                        del self.active_orders[order_id]
                        
                        # Update position and balance
                        if order.side == 'buy':
                            self.position += order.amount
                            self.balance -= order.amount * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            self.position -= order.amount
                            self.balance += order.amount * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    self.position += order.filled
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= order.filled
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def monitor_order(self, order_id: str, timeout: float = 30.0) -> AsyncGenerator[Dict, None]:
        """Monitor order status until it's filled or canceled"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Update mock data in test mode
                if self.test_mode:
                    self._update_mock_data()
                
                status = await self.check_order_status(order_id)
                yield status
                
                if status['status'] in [
                    OrderStatus.CLOSED,
                    OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    OrderStatus.EXPIRED,
                    OrderStatus.REJECTED
                ]:
                    break
                
                # Check timeout
                if (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() > timeout:
                    logger.warning(f"Order {order_id} monitoring timed out")
                    # Cancel order on timeout
                    await self.cancel_order(order_id)
                    break
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)  # Increased sleep time
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error monitoring order {order_id}: {e}")
                break

class LiveTradingEnvironment(gym.Env):
    def __init__(
        self,
        symbol: str = "BTC/USDT",
        initial_balance: float = 10000.0,
        trading_fee: float = 0.001,
        window_size: int = 60,
        exchange_id: str = "binance",
        max_data_points: int = 1000,
        test_mode: bool = False,
        retry_attempts: int = 3,
        retry_delay: float = 1.0,
        websocket: Optional[WebSocketLoader] = None,
        exchange: Optional[ccxt.Exchange] = None,
    ):
        super(LiveTradingEnvironment, self).__init__()

        self.symbol = symbol
        self.test_mode = test_mode
        self.retry_attempts = retry_attempts
        self.retry_delay = retry_delay

        # Initialize exchange
        if exchange is not None:
            self.exchange = exchange
        elif not test_mode:
            self.exchange = getattr(ccxt, exchange_id)()
        
        # Initialize WebSocket loader
        if websocket is not None:
            self.websocket = websocket
        else:
            self.websocket = WebSocketLoader(
                exchange_id=exchange_id,
                symbol=symbol,
                max_data_points=max_data_points,
            )

        # Trading parameters
        self.initial_balance = initial_balance
        self.trading_fee = trading_fee
        self.window_size = window_size

        # Action and observation spaces
        self.action_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-1, high=1, shape=(1,), dtype=np.float32
        )
        n_features = 13  # Update to match actual feature count
        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-np.inf,
            high=np.inf,
            shape=(window_size, n_features),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        # Order management
        self.active_orders: Dict[str, Order] = {}
        self.filled_orders: List[Order] = []
        self.canceled_orders: List[Order] = []
        
        # Rate limiting
        self.last_api_call = datetime.min
        self.rate_limit_reset = 0.0
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()

    def _initialize_state(self):
        """Initialize environment state"""
        self.balance = self.initial_balance
        self.position = 0.0
        self.trades = []
        self.active_orders = {}
        self.filled_orders = []
        self.canceled_orders = []
        self._last_portfolio_value = self.initial_balance
        self._last_price = 100.0  # Initial price for test mode
        
        # Initialize mock data for testing
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()

    def _update_mock_data(self):
        """Update mock market data for testing"""
        if not self.test_mode:
            return
            
        # Simulate price movement
        price_change = np.random.normal(0, 1.0)
        self._last_price *= (1 + price_change * 0.01)
        
        self.websocket.add_mock_data({
            'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
            'price': self._last_price,
            'volume': 1000.0,
            'bid': self._last_price * 0.995,
            'ask': self._last_price * 1.005,
            'bid_volume': 500.0,
            'ask_volume': 500.0
        })

    @property
    def portfolio_value(self) -> float:
        """Calculate current portfolio value"""
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        position_value = self.position * current_price
        return self.balance + position_value

    async def create_order(self, side: str, amount: float, price: float) -> str:
        """Create a new order"""
        for attempt in range(self.retry_attempts):
            try:
                if self.test_mode:
                    order_id = f"test_order_{len(self.active_orders)}"
                    order = Order(
                        id=order_id,
                        symbol=self.symbol,
                        type="limit",
                        side=side,
                        amount=amount,
                        price=price,
                        status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                    )
                    self.active_orders[order_id] = order
                    return order_id
                
                # Call exchange API
                order = await self.exchange.create_order(
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price
                )
                
                # Store order details
                order_id = order['id']
                order_obj = Order(
                    id=order_id,
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price,
                    status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                )
                self.active_orders[order_id] = order_obj
                return order_id
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error creating order (attempt {attempt + 1}/{self.retry_attempts}): {e}")
                if attempt < self.retry_attempts - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self.retry_delay)
                else:
                    raise

    async def cancel_order(self, order_id: str) -> bool:
        """Cancel an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return False
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
                self.canceled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                return True
            
            # Call exchange API
            await self.exchange.cancel_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
            self.canceled_orders.append(order)
            del self.active_orders[order_id]
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error canceling order {order_id}: {e}")
            return False

    async def check_order_status(self, order_id: str) -> Dict:
        """Check the status of an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return {
                    'id': order_id,
                    'status': OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    'filled': 0.0,
                    'remaining': 0.0
                }
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Simulate order filling in test mode
                if order.status == OrderStatus.OPEN:
                    current_price = self._last_price
                    if (order.side == 'buy' and current_price <= order.price) or \
                       (order.side == 'sell' and current_price >= order.price):
                        order.filled = order.amount
                        order.remaining = 0.0
                        order.status = OrderStatus.CLOSED
                        self.filled_orders.append(order)
                        del self.active_orders[order_id]
                        
                        # Update position and balance
                        if order.side == 'buy':
                            self.position += order.amount
                            self.balance -= order.amount * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            self.position -= order.amount
                            self.balance += order.amount * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    self.position += order.filled
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= order.filled
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def monitor_order(self, order_id: str, timeout: float = 30.0) -> AsyncGenerator[Dict, None]:
        """Monitor order status until it's filled or canceled"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Update mock data in test mode
                if self.test_mode:
                    self._update_mock_data()
                
                status = await self.check_order_status(order_id)
                yield status
                
                if status['status'] in [
                    OrderStatus.CLOSED,
                    OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    OrderStatus.EXPIRED,
                    OrderStatus.REJECTED
                ]:
                    break
                
                # Check timeout
                if (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() > timeout:
                    logger.warning(f"Order {order_id} monitoring timed out")
                    # Cancel order on timeout
                    await self.cancel_order(order_id)
                    break
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)  # Increased sleep time
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error monitoring order {order_id}: {e}")
                break

    async def reset(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]:
        """Reset the environment"""
        self._initialize_state()
        
        # Get initial observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value
        }
        
        return observation, info

    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float, bool, bool, Dict]:
        """Execute one step in the environment"""
        # Get current price
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        
        # Calculate order amount based on action
        action_value = float(action[0])  # Convert to float
        position_change = action_value * 0.1  # Scale action to position change
        
        # Create and monitor order
        if position_change > 0:
            # Buy order
            amount = abs(position_change)
            price = current_price * 1.001  # Slightly above market price
            order_id = await self.create_order('buy', amount, price)
        else:
            # Sell order
            amount = abs(position_change)
            price = current_price * 0.999  # Slightly below market price
            order_id = await self.create_order('sell', amount, price)
        
        # Monitor order
        async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
            if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                break
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward
        portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = portfolio_value
        
        # Update info
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value
        }
        
        # Check if episode is done
        done = False  # Implement your own termination conditions
        truncated = False
        
        return observation, reward, done, truncated, info

    async def _get_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get current observation"""
        # Implement your observation logic here
        return np.zeros((self.window_size, 13))  # Placeholder

                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    self.position += order.filled
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= order.filled
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def monitor_order(self, order_id: str, timeout: float = 30.0) -> AsyncGenerator[Dict, None]:
        """Monitor order status until it's filled or canceled"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Update mock data in test mode
                if self.test_mode:
                    self._update_mock_data()
                
                status = await self.check_order_status(order_id)
                yield status
                
                if status['status'] in [
                    OrderStatus.CLOSED,
                    OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    OrderStatus.EXPIRED,
                    OrderStatus.REJECTED
                ]:
                    break
                
                # Check timeout
                if (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() > timeout:
                    logger.warning(f"Order {order_id} monitoring timed out")
                    # Cancel order on timeout
                    await self.cancel_order(order_id)
                    break
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)  # Increased sleep time
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error monitoring order {order_id}: {e}")
                break

    async def reset(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]:
        """Reset the environment"""
        self._initialize_state()
        
        # Get initial observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value
        }
        
        return observation, info

    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float, bool, bool, Dict]:
        """Execute one step in the environment"""
        # Get current price
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        
        # Calculate order amount based on action
        action_value = float(action[0])  # Convert to float
        position_change = action_value * 0.05  # Reduced position change
        
        # Create and monitor order
        if abs(position_change) > 0.001:  # Minimum position change threshold
            if position_change > 0:
                # Buy order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 1.001  # Slightly above market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('buy', amount, price)
            else:
                # Sell order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 0.999  # Slightly below market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('sell', amount, price)
            
            # Monitor order
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                    break
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward
        portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = portfolio_value
        
        # Update info
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value
        }
        
        # Check if episode is done
        done = False  # Implement your own termination conditions
        truncated = False
        
        return observation, reward, done, truncated, info

    async def _get_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get current observation"""
        if self.test_mode:
            # Use mock data
            df = self.websocket._current_data
        else:
            # Get real market data
            df = self.websocket.get_current_data()
        
        # Ensure we have enough data points
        if len(df) < self.window_size:
            # Pad with zeros if necessary
            padding = pd.DataFrame(
                np.zeros((self.window_size - len(df), len(df.columns))),
                columns=df.columns
            )
            df = pd.concat([padding, df])
        
        # Extract features
        features = []
        for col in df.columns:
            if col != 'timestamp':
                features.append(df[col].values)
        
        # Add portfolio information
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.position))
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.balance / self.initial_balance))
        
        # Convert to numpy array
        observation = np.array(features).T
        return observation.astype(np.float32)

    async def cleanup(self):
        """Cleanup resources"""
        # Cancel all active orders
        for order_id in list(self.active_orders.keys()):
            await self.cancel_order(order_id)
        
        # Close exchange connection
        if not self.test_mode and hasattr(self, 'exchange'):
            await self.exchange.close()
        
        # Stop WebSocket
        if hasattr(self, 'websocket'):
            await self.websocket.stop()

    def render(self):
        """Render the environment"""
        pass
```

class LiveTradingEnvironment(gym.Env):
    def __init__(
        self,
        symbol: str = "BTC/USDT",
        initial_balance: float = 10000.0,
        trading_fee: float = 0.001,
        window_size: int = 60,
        exchange_id: str = "binance",
        max_data_points: int = 1000,
        test_mode: bool = False,
        retry_attempts: int = 3,
        retry_delay: float = 1.0,
        websocket: Optional[WebSocketLoader] = None,
        exchange: Optional[ccxt.Exchange] = None,
    ):
        super(LiveTradingEnvironment, self).__init__()

        self.symbol = symbol
        self.test_mode = test_mode
        self.retry_attempts = retry_attempts
        self.retry_delay = retry_delay

        # Initialize exchange
        if exchange is not None:
            self.exchange = exchange
        elif not test_mode:
            self.exchange = getattr(ccxt, exchange_id)()
        
        # Initialize WebSocket loader
        if websocket is not None:
            self.websocket = websocket
        else:
            self.websocket = WebSocketLoader(
                exchange_id=exchange_id,
                symbol=symbol,
                max_data_points=max_data_points,
            )

        # Trading parameters
        self.initial_balance = initial_balance
        self.trading_fee = trading_fee
        self.window_size = window_size

        # Action and observation spaces
        self.action_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-1, high=1, shape=(1,), dtype=np.float32
        )
        self.n_features = 13  # OHLCV (5) + market depth (4) + technical (2) + portfolio (2)
        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-np.inf,
            high=np.inf,
            shape=(window_size, self.n_features),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        # Order management
        self.active_orders: Dict[str, Order] = {}
        self.filled_orders: List[Order] = []
        self.canceled_orders: List[Order] = []
        
        # Rate limiting
        self.last_api_call = datetime.min
        self.rate_limit_reset = 0.0
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()

    def _initialize_state(self):
        """Initialize environment state"""
        self.balance = self.initial_balance
        self.position = 0.0
        self.trades = []
        self.active_orders = {}
        self.filled_orders = []
        self.canceled_orders = []
        self._last_portfolio_value = self.initial_balance
        self._last_price = 100.0  # Initial price for test mode
        
        # Initialize mock data for testing
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()

    def _update_mock_data(self):
        """Update mock market data for testing"""
        if not self.test_mode:
            return
            
        # Simulate price movement
        price_change = np.random.normal(0, 1.0)
        self._last_price *= (1 + price_change * 0.01)
        
        # Create mock market data
        mock_data = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
            'open': self._last_price * 0.999,
            'high': self._last_price * 1.001,
            'low': self._last_price * 0.998,
            'close': self._last_price,
            'volume': 1000.0,
            'bid': self._last_price * 0.995,
            'ask': self._last_price * 1.005,
            'bid_volume': 500.0,
            'ask_volume': 500.0,
            'rsi': 50.0,  # Mock RSI
            'macd': 0.0,  # Mock MACD
        }
        
        # Update WebSocket data
        self.websocket.add_mock_data(mock_data)
        
        # Update current data
        df = pd.DataFrame([mock_data])
        df.set_index('timestamp', inplace=True)
        self.websocket._current_data = df

    @property
    def portfolio_value(self) -> float:
        """Calculate current portfolio value"""
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        position_value = self.position * current_price
        return self.balance + position_value

    async def create_order(self, side: str, amount: float, price: float) -> str:
        """Create a new order"""
        for attempt in range(self.retry_attempts):
            try:
                if self.test_mode:
                    order_id = f"test_order_{len(self.active_orders)}"
                    order = Order(
                        id=order_id,
                        symbol=self.symbol,
                        type="limit",
                        side=side,
                        amount=amount,
                        price=price,
                        status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                    )
                    self.active_orders[order_id] = order
                    return order_id
                
                # Call exchange API
                order = await self.exchange.create_order(
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price
                )
                
                # Store order details
                order_id = order['id']
                order_obj = Order(
                    id=order_id,
                    symbol=self.symbol,
                    type="limit",
                    side=side,
                    amount=amount,
                    price=price,
                    status=OrderStatus.OPEN
                )
                self.active_orders[order_id] = order_obj
                return order_id
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error creating order (attempt {attempt + 1}/{self.retry_attempts}): {e}")
                if attempt < self.retry_attempts - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self.retry_delay)
                else:
                    raise

    async def cancel_order(self, order_id: str) -> bool:
        """Cancel an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return False
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
                self.canceled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                return True
            
            # Call exchange API
            await self.exchange.cancel_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELED
            self.canceled_orders.append(order)
            del self.active_orders[order_id]
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error canceling order {order_id}: {e}")
            return False

    async def check_order_status(self, order_id: str) -> Dict:
        """Check the status of an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return {
                    'id': order_id,
                    'status': OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    'filled': 0.0,
                    'remaining': 0.0
                }
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Simulate order filling in test mode
                if order.status == OrderStatus.OPEN:
                    current_price = self._last_price
                    if (order.side == 'buy' and current_price <= order.price) or \
                       (order.side == 'sell' and current_price >= order.price):
                        order.filled = order.amount
                        order.remaining = 0.0
                        order.status = OrderStatus.CLOSED
                        self.filled_orders.append(order)
                        del self.active_orders[order_id]
                        
                        # Update position and balance
                        if order.side == 'buy':
                            self.position += order.amount
                            self.balance -= order.amount * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            self.position -= order.amount
                            self.balance += order.amount * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    self.position += order.filled
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= order.filled
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def monitor_order(self, order_id: str, timeout: float = 30.0) -> AsyncGenerator[Dict, None]:
        """Monitor order status until it's filled or canceled"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Update mock data in test mode
                if self.test_mode:
                    self._update_mock_data()
                
                status = await self.check_order_status(order_id)
                yield status
                
                if status['status'] in [
                    OrderStatus.CLOSED,
                    OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    OrderStatus.EXPIRED,
                    OrderStatus.REJECTED
                ]:
                    break
                
                # Check timeout
                if (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds() > timeout:
                    logger.warning(f"Order {order_id} monitoring timed out")
                    # Cancel order on timeout
                    await self.cancel_order(order_id)
                    break
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)  # Increased sleep time
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error monitoring order {order_id}: {e}")
                break

    async def reset(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]:
        """Reset the environment"""
        # Cancel all active orders
        await self.cancel_all_orders()
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()
        
        # Get initial observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value
        }
        
        return observation, info

    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float, bool, bool, Dict]:
        """Execute one step in the environment"""
        # Get current price
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        
        # Calculate order amount based on action
        action_value = float(action[0])  # Convert to float
        position_change = action_value * 0.025  # Reduced position change
        
        # Create and monitor order
        if abs(position_change) > 0.001:  # Minimum position change threshold
            if position_change > 0:
                # Buy order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 1.001  # Slightly above market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('buy', amount, price)
            else:
                # Sell order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 0.999  # Slightly below market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('sell', amount, price)
            
            # Monitor order
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                    break
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward
        portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = portfolio_value
        
        # Update info
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value
        }
        
        # Check if episode is done
        done = False  # Implement your own termination conditions
        truncated = False
        
        return observation, reward, done, truncated, info

    async def _get_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get current observation"""
        if self.test_mode:
            # Use mock data
            df = self.websocket._current_data
        else:
            # Get real market data
            df = self.websocket.get_current_data()
        
        # Ensure we have enough data points
        if len(df) < self.window_size:
            # Pad with zeros if necessary
            padding = pd.DataFrame(
                np.zeros((self.window_size - len(df), len(df.columns))),
                columns=df.columns
            )
            df = pd.concat([padding, df])
        
        # Extract features
        features = []
        # OHLCV
        for col in ['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Market depth
        for col in ['bid', 'ask', 'bid_volume', 'ask_volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Technical indicators
        for col in ['rsi', 'macd']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Portfolio information
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.position))
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.balance / self.initial_balance))
        
        # Convert to numpy array
        observation = np.array(features).T
        return observation.astype(np.float32)

    async def cancel_all_orders(self) -> List[str]:
        """Cancel all active orders"""
        canceled_orders = []
        for order_id in list(self.active_orders.keys()):
            if await self.cancel_order(order_id):
                canceled_orders.append(order_id)
        return canceled_orders

    async def cleanup(self):
        """Cleanup resources"""
        # Cancel all active orders
        await self.cancel_all_orders()
        
        # Close exchange connection
        if not self.test_mode and hasattr(self, 'exchange'):
            await self.exchange.close()
        
        # Stop WebSocket
        if hasattr(self, 'websocket'):
            await self.websocket.stop()

    def render(self):
        """Render the environment"""
        pass

        action_value = float(action[0])  # Convert to float
        position_change = action_value * 0.01  # Further reduced position change
        
        # Create and monitor order
        if abs(position_change) > 0.001:  # Minimum position change threshold
            if position_change > 0:
                # Buy order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 1.001  # Slightly above market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('buy', amount, price)
            else:
                # Sell order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 0.999  # Slightly below market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('sell', amount, price)
            
            # Monitor order
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                    break
        
        # Update mock data in test mode
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward
        portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = portfolio_value
        
        # Update info
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value
        }
        
        # Check if episode is done
        done = False  # Implement your own termination conditions
        truncated = False
        
        return observation, reward, done, truncated, info

    async def reset(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]:
        """Reset the environment"""
        # Cancel all active orders
        await self.cancel_all_orders()
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()
        
        # Update mock data in test mode
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()
        
        # Get initial observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': self.position,
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value
        }
        
        return observation, info

    def _update_mock_data(self):
        """Update mock market data for testing"""
        if not self.test_mode:
            return
            
        # Simulate price movement
        price_change = np.random.normal(0, 1.0)
        self._last_price *= (1 + price_change * 0.01)
        
        # Create mock market data
        mock_data = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
            'open': self._last_price * 0.999,
            'high': self._last_price * 1.001,
            'low': self._last_price * 0.998,
            'close': self._last_price,
            'volume': 1000.0,
            'bid': self._last_price * 0.995,
            'ask': self._last_price * 1.005,
            'bid_volume': 500.0,
            'ask_volume': 500.0,
            'rsi': 50.0 + price_change * 5.0,  # Mock RSI with some variation
            'macd': price_change * 0.1,  # Mock MACD with some variation
        }
        
        # Update WebSocket data
        self.websocket.add_mock_data(mock_data)
        
        # Update current data
        df = pd.DataFrame([mock_data])
        df.set_index('timestamp', inplace=True)
        self.websocket._current_data = df

    @property
    def portfolio_value(self) -> float:
        """Calculate current portfolio value"""
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        position_value = self.position * current_price
        return self.balance + position_value

    async def check_order_status(self, order_id: str) -> Dict:
        """Check the status of an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return {
                    'id': order_id,
                    'status': OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    'filled': 0.0,
                    'remaining': 0.0
                }
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Simulate order filling in test mode
                if order.status == OrderStatus.OPEN:
                    current_price = self._last_price
                    if (order.side == 'buy' and current_price <= order.price) or \
                       (order.side == 'sell' and current_price >= order.price):
                        # Partial fill simulation
                        fill_ratio = np.random.uniform(0.5, 1.0)
                        order.filled = order.amount * fill_ratio
                        order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
                        order.status = OrderStatus.CLOSED
                        self.filled_orders.append(order)
                        del self.active_orders[order_id]
                        
                        # Update position and balance
                        if order.side == 'buy':
                            self.position += order.filled
                            self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            self.position -= order.filled
                            self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    self.position += order.filled
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    self.position -= order.filled
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def _get_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get current observation"""
        if self.test_mode:
            # Use mock data
            df = self.websocket._current_data
        else:
            # Get real market data
            df = self.websocket.get_current_data()
        
        # Ensure we have enough data points
        if len(df) < self.window_size:
            # Pad with zeros if necessary
            padding = pd.DataFrame(
                np.zeros((self.window_size - len(df), len(df.columns))),
                columns=df.columns
            )
            df = pd.concat([padding, df])
        
        # Extract features
        features = []
        # OHLCV
        for col in ['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Market depth
        for col in ['bid', 'ask', 'bid_volume', 'ask_volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Technical indicators
        for col in ['rsi', 'macd']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Portfolio information
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.position))
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.balance / self.initial_balance))
        
        # Convert to numpy array
        observation = np.array(features).T
        return observation.astype(np.float32)

            else:

            # Monitor order
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                    break
        
        # Update mock data in test mode
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward

            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value

                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

    async def _get_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get current observation"""
        if self.test_mode:
            # Use mock data
            df = self.websocket._current_data
        else:
            # Get real market data
            df = self.websocket.get_current_data()
        
        # Ensure we have enough data points
        if len(df) < self.window_size:
            # Pad with zeros if necessary
            padding = pd.DataFrame(
                np.zeros((self.window_size - len(df), len(df.columns))),
                columns=df.columns
            )
            df = pd.concat([padding, df])
        
        # Extract features
        features = []
        # OHLCV
        for col in ['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Market depth
        for col in ['bid', 'ask', 'bid_volume', 'ask_volume']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Technical indicators
        for col in ['rsi', 'macd']:
            features.append(df[col].values)
        # Portfolio information
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.position))
        features.append(np.full(len(df), self.balance / self.initial_balance))
        
        # Convert to numpy array
        observation = np.array(features).T
        return observation.astype(np.float32)

    async def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float, bool, bool, Dict]:
        """Execute one step in the environment"""
        # Get current price
        current_price = self._last_price if self.test_mode else self.websocket.get_latest_price()
        
        # Calculate order amount based on action
        action_value = float(action[0])  # Convert to float
        position_change = action_value * 0.01  # Further reduced position change
        
        # Create and monitor order
        if abs(position_change) > 0.001:  # Minimum position change threshold
            if position_change > 0:
                # Buy order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 1.001  # Slightly above market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('buy', amount, price)
            else:
                # Sell order
                amount = abs(position_change)
                price = current_price * 0.999  # Slightly below market price
                order_id = await self.create_order('sell', amount, price)
            
            # Monitor order
            async for status in self.monitor_order(order_id):
                if status['status'] in [OrderStatus.CLOSED, OrderStatus.CANCELED]:
                    break
        
        # Update mock data in test mode
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()
        
        # Get new observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        
        # Calculate reward
        portfolio_value = self.portfolio_value
        reward = (portfolio_value - self._last_portfolio_value) / self._last_portfolio_value
        self._last_portfolio_value = portfolio_value
        
        # Update info
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': 0.0 if abs(self.position) < 1e-6 else round(self.position, 8),  # Handle small positions
            'portfolio_value': portfolio_value
        }
        
        # Check if episode is done
        done = False  # Implement your own termination conditions
        truncated = False
        
        return observation, reward, done, truncated, info

    async def reset(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]:
        """Reset the environment"""
        # Cancel all active orders
        await self.cancel_all_orders()
        
        # Initialize state
        self._initialize_state()
        
        # Update mock data in test mode
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()
        
        # Get initial observation
        observation = await self._get_observation()
        info = {
            'balance': self.balance,
            'position': 0.0 if abs(self.position) < 1e-6 else round(self.position, 8),  # Handle small positions
            'portfolio_value': self.portfolio_value
        }
        
        return observation, info

    def _initialize_state(self):
        """Initialize environment state"""
        self.balance = self.initial_balance
        self.position = 0.0
        self.trades = []
        self.active_orders = {}
        self.filled_orders = []
        self.canceled_orders = []
        self._last_portfolio_value = self.initial_balance
        self._last_price = 100.0  # Initial price for test mode
        
        # Initialize mock data for testing
        if self.test_mode:
            self._update_mock_data()

    def _update_mock_data(self):
        """Update mock market data for testing"""
        if not self.test_mode:
            return
            
        # Simulate price movement
        price_change = np.random.normal(0, 1.0)
        self._last_price *= (1 + price_change * 0.01)
        
        # Create mock market data
        mock_data = {
            'timestamp': datetime.now().timestamp(),
            'open': self._last_price * 0.999,
            'high': self._last_price * 1.001,
            'low': self._last_price * 0.998,
            'close': self._last_price,
            'volume': 1000.0,
            'bid': self._last_price * 0.995,
            'ask': self._last_price * 1.005,
            'bid_volume': 500.0,
            'ask_volume': 500.0,
            'rsi': 50.0 + price_change * 5.0,  # Mock RSI with some variation
            'macd': price_change * 0.1,  # Mock MACD with some variation
        }
        
        # Update WebSocket data
        self.websocket.add_mock_data(mock_data)
        
        # Update current data
        df = pd.DataFrame([mock_data])
        df.set_index('timestamp', inplace=True)
        self.websocket._current_data = df

    async def check_order_status(self, order_id: str) -> Dict:
        """Check the status of an order"""
        try:
            if order_id not in self.active_orders:
                return {
                    'id': order_id,
                    'status': OrderStatus.CANCELED,
                    'filled': 0.0,
                    'remaining': 0.0
                }
            
            if self.test_mode:
                order = self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Simulate order filling in test mode
                if order.status == OrderStatus.OPEN:
                    current_price = self._last_price
                    if (order.side == 'buy' and current_price <= order.price) or \
                       (order.side == 'sell' and current_price >= order.price):
                        # Partial fill simulation
                        fill_ratio = np.random.uniform(0.5, 1.0)
                        order.filled = order.amount * fill_ratio
                        order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
                        order.status = OrderStatus.CLOSED
                        self.filled_orders.append(order)
                        del self.active_orders[order_id]
                        
                        # Update position and balance
                        if order.side == 'buy':
                            new_position = self.position + order.filled
                            self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-6 else round(new_position, 8)
                            self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            new_position = self.position - order.filled
                            self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-6 else round(new_position, 8)
                            self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    new_position = self.position + order.filled
                    self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-6 else round(new_position, 8)
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    new_position = self.position - order.filled
                    self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-6 else round(new_position, 8)
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise

                            self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-5 else round(new_position, 8)
                            self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                        else:
                            new_position = self.position - order.filled
                            self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-5 else round(new_position, 8)
                            self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
                
                return {
                    'id': order.id,
                    'status': order.status,
                    'filled': order.filled,
                    'remaining': order.remaining
                }
            
            # Call exchange API
            order_info = await self.exchange.fetch_order(order_id, self.symbol)
            
            # Update order status
            order = self.active_orders[order_id]
            order.status = order_info['status']
            order.filled = order_info['filled']
            order.remaining = order.amount - order.filled
            
            if order.status == OrderStatus.CLOSED:
                self.filled_orders.append(order)
                del self.active_orders[order_id]
                
                # Update position and balance
                if order.side == 'buy':
                    new_position = self.position + order.filled
                    self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-5 else round(new_position, 8)
                    self.balance -= order.filled * order.price * (1 + self.trading_fee)
                else:
                    new_position = self.position - order.filled
                    self.position = 0.0 if abs(new_position) < 1e-5 else round(new_position, 8)
                    self.balance += order.filled * order.price * (1 - self.trading_fee)
            
            return {
                'id': order.id,
                'status': order.status,
                'filled': order.filled,
                'remaining': order.remaining
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error checking order {order_id}: {e}")
            raise
